Technical reconnaissance is in the repo. No application code, no frontend scaffold, no scope freeze.

| File | What it is |
| --- | --- |
| `research/HAR_ANALYSIS.md` | Evidence from `higgsfield.ai.har` only, with CONFIRMED / STRONGLY INFERRED / UNKNOWN |
| `research/FLOW_MAP.md` | Walkthrough tracks for every assigned surface, plus extras the HAR named |
| `research/RESEARCH_PLAN.md` | Ordered live sessions and screenshot filenames |

The HAR is **one unauthenticated Chrome load of `/`** (~6 minutes, 1,859 requests). It maps the platform. It does not replace using the product.

### 1. Strongest findings

- **Stack:** React + **TanStack Router** SSR (`$tsr` / `$_TSR`), assets on `assets.higgsfield.ai/tanstack/`. Vite is strongly inferred. Tailwind + a **Quanta** theme system (`q-*`, `hf:quanta:theme-*`).
- **Auth:** **Clerk** hosted at `clerk.higgsfield.ai` (email/password + Google/Apple/GitHub/Microsoft). Home is public. Product user API is not: `GET fnf-api-gw.higgsfield.ai/fnf/user` → **401**.
- **APIs seen:** `fnf-api-gw` (user, geo meta PK, promo windows, Stripe-shaped **Basic/Pro/Max/Team/Scale** plans), `cms.higgsfield.ai` (notices + explore presets), GrowthBook, PostHog via `/api/i/`.
- **Media:** Cloudflare image resize, `cdn.higgsfield.ai` (cards, viral_hub, **HLS 480p**), `images.higgs.ai` proxy, CloudFront user objects.
- **Nav (logged-out home):** Image `/ai/image?model=gpt_image_2`, Video `/ai/video`, Audio `/audio`, Effects `/effects/use`, **Cinema Studio → `/generate`**, Marketing Studio, Supercomputer, **Edit → `/layers`**, Canvas, Community. Recreate on cards → `/effects/use/{slug}`.
- **No generation ran.** All POSTs were analytics/Sentry/Cloudflare. Job types appear only in CMS notices (Kling, Veo, Sora, Seedance, …).
- **365 routes** in `/api/route-preload-manifest`, and ~1,187 JS chunks prefetched while staying on `/`.

### 2. Biggest unknowns

- Logged-in IA: library/history, credits, workspaces (`X-Fnf-Workspace-Id` is allowed by CORS, never sent here).
- Create/job APIs, polling, uploads, signed storage.
- Whether `/generate`, `/cinematic-video-generator`, and `/_private/cinema-studio` are the same Cinema Studio.
- Edit vs Layers vs `video-editor`.
- Supercomputer vs `/gpt-astra` vs `/mcp`.
- Clerk `after_sign_in_url` is `beta.higgsfield.ai` — unproven in a real login.
- Cookies were stripped from the HAR.

Full gap list is in `HAR_ANALYSIS.md` §17.

### 3. Exact live walkthrough next

Do **Session 1** in `RESEARCH_PLAN.md` first, still logged out: click every header item, screenshot with the URL bar, and confirm Cinema → `/generate` and Edit → `/layers`. Then **Session 2** (sign in, capture `/fnf/user` succeeding). Then **Session 3** on the same surfaces logged in, plus one cheap/gated generate only if you need in-progress UI.

Do not start React or pick rebuild scope until those notes exist.

This pass was **logged out only**. Headless inspection is an inventory, not a walkthrough. Generate, upload, hover, drag, Canvas, credits, and Clerk UI are still **MANUAL VERIFICATION REQUIRED**.

### 1. Highest-value discoveries

- **Create is a small number of shells, not a large number of products.** Image, Video, and Audio share one generator pattern. Models are `?model=` / Change-picker engines (Genjutsu is just Video with `?model=genjutsu`).
- **Cinema `/generate` is not the marketing page.** `/generate` is Cinema Studio 4.0. `/cinematic-video-generator` is SEO. Footer often uses intro URLs; nav uses the workspace.
- **Apps are one storefront.** `/apps` has 8 category routes and a shared card → Try now → SEO + upload + Generate shell. Face Swap (two photos) vs Relight (lighting drag) shows what actually differs. Relight also appears as a Layers capability.
- **`/effects/use` is already a composer**, default **FLOATING FALL**, with Change → preset picker — not a catalog landing. Recreate on home (HAR) vs Recreate on Supercomputer/MCP may not be the same destination.
- **Library is public and empty logged out** (`/library/all`). **Profile is gated** (`/profile` → `/auth/sign-in`). Supercomputer, Canvas, Layers, and Marketing all load logged out with empty personal rails. Credit numbers show on Generate **before** login.
- **3D Jutsu names Seedance 2.5 as the export engine** — another model-inside-workspace, not a new flow family.

### 2. Patterns that cut how many flows you need to test

- **One Image + one Video + one Audio generate (or gate)** covers the core composer. After that, only record model/tab deltas.
- **Do not walk every model or every app.** Sample 2–3 app input types (two-image, lighting/drag, maybe product-link). Sample the Video Change list once.
- **One Effects Generate** (the “1 FREE LEFT” path is the obvious cheap check) likely stands in for many presets.
- **One successful job, then trace where it lands** — Library vs History vs Cinema/Marketing rails vs Supercomputer chat — answers the asset question better than opening every empty list now.
- **Cinema vs Marketing** share a studio rail; **Supercomputer vs MCP** share Recreate showcases. Test one of each pair, then look for deltas.

### 3. Next 5 manual checks

1. **Hover Image and Video mega-menus** (Video did not expand on click in headless) and screenshot Features vs Models vs any Video menu.
2. **Click Login and Sign up** (do not skip Clerk). Headless `/auth/sign-in` did not hydrate — confirm hosted vs embedded, OAuth, and where you land after.
3. **Run one cheap/free Generate** (Effects “1 FREE LEFT” or a gated click) and capture Clerk vs paywall vs job UI — not a full paid run unless you need in-progress/result chrome.
4. **After that job (or after login), open Library, generator History, and one studio rail** and see which actually shows the result.
5. **Recreate from three places:** home/community card, Supercomputer showcase, Effects Change preset — and note whether they all hit `/effects/use/{slug}` or split into Supercomputer/apps.

Yes. Headless **skipped those URLs**. Clicking Video went to `/ai/video` without opening the menu; Audio stayed collapsed. These hrefs only exist because you hovered and pasted the DOM.

They’re now in `PRODUCT_MAP.md`, `ROUTE_INVENTORY.md`, and `REPEATED_PATTERNS.md`. `FLOW_MAP.md` is unchanged.

**Same pattern as Image:** Features (destinations) vs Models (engines). Analytics still say `Motions page`.

**Video Features that headless never saw:** `/faceless-studio`, `/shorts-studio`, `/explainer`, `/mixed-media`, `/ai/video/edit`, `/ai/video/reframe`, `/ai/video/motion`, `/apps/video-relight`, `/apps/video-rehex`, `/lipsync-studio`, Draw to Video/Edit query flags, UGC Factory (`/lipsync-studio?ugc-studio=new`). Cinema from this menu is `/generate?mode=video`, not bare `/generate`.

**Do not walk every Video Model.** The menu is a shortlist. Kling Motion Control and Omni Edit are **routes**, not just `?model=`. DOP is `model=standard`. Video Relight ≠ Image Relight. Video Upscale is the same `/upscale` as Image.

**Audio:** TTS / Voice Change / Translate are `voiceMode`. Models (Seed Audio, Eleven v3, Qwen, MiniMax, Seed Speech) all open **Voiceover** plus `audioModel=`. That resolves the HAR logo vs composer conflict: the engines are in the menu, not missing.

Plugins mega-menu is still uncaptured. The new studio URLs are inventoried, not opened — sample those only if you care about that workspace, not because each menu row is a separate flow.

Higgsfield is a small set of shells with many labeled doors, not 80 separate products. The map is in `research/PRODUCT_FLOW_GRAPH.md`.

**About 18 distinct workflow families remain** after collapsing models, tabs, modes, and duplicate menu rows.

### What actually exists

**Core generators (3):** Image `/ai/image`, Video `/ai/video`, Audio `/audio`.

**Packaged:** Apps storefront + Effects presets.

**Transforms:** Layers/Edit, Upscale (image and video on one page), plus Relight / Video Relight / Color Palette / Face Swap / Character Swap as Apps.

**Workspaces:** Cinema, Marketing, Canvas, Moodboard, Soul ID Character, AI Influencer, Faceless, Shorts, Explainer, Mixed Media, Lipsync, 3D Jutsu.

**Agentic:** Supercomputer, MCP, ChatGPT Plugin.

**Integrations:** six host-install pages (Adobe / Figma / Blender) plus a distinct Minecraft mod. Header API goes to `console.higgsfield.ai`.

**Discovery / storage / social:** Explore, Community, Creation Hub `/flow` (live catalog, not in the header), Library, History, gated Profile.

### Duplicate doors (verified)

| Menu item | Actually |
|---|---|
| Header Image vs Create Image | Same Image shell, different default `model` |
| Photodump | Image + Soul 2.0 + modal |
| Genjutsu | Video shell + specialized motion-transfer UI |
| Cinematic Cameras / Video Cinema | Cinema `/generate` (query often stripped; Image/Video are Cinema tabs) |
| Topaz + Image Upscale + Video Upscale | `/upscale` |
| Draw to Video / Draw to Edit | Video + inpaint overlay |
| Edit / Motion / Reframe | Video tabs/routes, not new products |
| UGC Factory | Lipsync + `ugc-studio=true` |
| Click to Ad | Redirects to Marketing `?tool=url-to-ad` |
| Audio providers | `audioModel` on TTS only; `voiceMode` is the workflow |
| Character Swap vs Soul ID Character | App vs identity workspace |
| Image Relight vs Video Relight | Two different Apps (SEO titles are swapped with Color Palette) |
| Footer Creator Hub | Help Center, **not** `/flow` |

### Plugins

They are **host integrations**, not web generators. Six share one install landing (“Higgsfield is now inside {host}”). Minecraft is a different mod page (NeoForge, in-world Supercomputer). ChatGPT Plugin and MCP stay out of that menu.

### Still unknown (needs you)

Auth/Clerk, whether Generate gates or spends, job → History → Library sync, Publish, Supercomputer Recreate → Explainer, Canvas execution, Mixed Media vs Effects, in-host plugin use.

### Shortest manual walk

Explore Recreate → `/flow` picker → one Image generate + one model switch → Video tabs (don’t pay three times) → Audio TTS then Voice Change → Effects free generate if you spend anything → Cinema New project → Marketing Product Link → Layers upload + Upscale glance → one App → Lipsync/UGC → one studio (Explainer or Shorts) → Supercomputer Recreate → Library + Community. One plugin landing on the web is enough.

No rebuild scope. Next step is the manual walk in `FLOW_MAP.md`, not more route discovery.

The final scope is in `research/IMPLEMENTATION_SCOPE.md`. The proposal already had the right loop (Explore → Recreate → Image/Video/Effects → gate). These are the corrections research forced.

**Missing from the core experience**
- `/effects/examples/:slug` — Home Effects cards open an example page, not the composer.
- Bare `/effects/use` — header Effects lands on the composer with a default preset.
- Recreate hydration — live logged-out Image Recreate often left the prompt empty; the rebuild should still fill prompt/settings, or Recreate is just a link.
- Genjutsu **Open preset** overlay on `/` — not a publication page.
- Library logged-out empty state — `/library/all` already loads public.
- Logged-out Like → auth modal (same Welcome modal as Generate).
- Seeded History on the generator shells, and a thin project page so Home project cards don’t 404.

**Poor value for the time**
- Marketing Studio on Home, with the workspace omitted and no Recreate on the inspected publication.
- Seedance 2.0 as a second Home gallery (same Video door as 2.5).
- Every `*-community` / Genjutsu-presets marketing landing.
- Full Video Edit + Motion composers (tabs of one shell, not products).
- Cully Hill Boys asset/act/shotlist/comment workspace.
- Publication Video / Upscale / Edit (those enter omitted shells).
- Full Image/Video Features mega-menu.

**Wrong shell assumptions**
- Soul Cinema is Image (`soul-cinematic`), not Cinema Studio.
- Genjutsu is Video + extra slots, not its own generator.
- Create / Edit / Motion are one Video shell; only Create needs to work.
- Models are `?model=` engines, not routes.
- Home galleries are discovery, not products.

**Smaller architecture**
- One generation record feeds Home, Community, Library, publication, and Recreate.
- Image and Video share primitives; Effects shares Generate/upload/gates only.
- One Auth modal and one Upgrade modal.
- View all goes to `/community` or the shell, never a new community product page.
- Reduced header Models list for in-scope engines only.

Omitted on purpose: Audio, Cinema, Marketing workspace, Canvas, Layers, Upscale, Supercomputer, plugins, Photodump, and extra models. Target remains about 14–16 hours, with polish beating more routes.

establish the decisions explicitly in this repo and under the current agent capture.

Use this architecture:

## Core stack

- React
- TypeScript
- Vite
- TanStack Router
- Tailwind CSS
- Radix primitives where they materially help accessibility/interaction
- Zustand for small shared client state

Do NOT use Next.js.

## State ownership

Follow this hierarchy strictly:

### 1. URL / product state → TanStack Router

Use route params/search params for state that identifies the product configuration or should survive navigation:

- selected model
- `recreateId`
- publication id
- effect slug
- meaningful generator mode
- routes/tabs when they represent navigation

Examples:

`/ai/image?model=soul-v2&recreateId=abc`

`/ai/video?model=genjutsu`

The URL is the source of truth for these values.

Do not duplicate URL state into Zustand.

### 2. Remote/server state → TanStack Query

TanStack Query is the preferred mechanism if we introduce actual async/server-style data.

However, this rebuild uses mostly seeded local data.

Do NOT install or use TanStack Query merely to wrap static mock arrays.

Only introduce Query when there is a real asynchronous/server-state use case.

### 3. Shared client state → Zustand

Use Zustand for small pieces of state that need to survive across unrelated routes/components.

Create a small app/demo store for things such as:

- mock authenticated user / auth status
- authenticated Like state/count overrides
- optional billing-cycle preference
- other genuinely cross-route demo state if discovered

Keep the store intentionally small.

Do NOT put everything into Zustand.

Do NOT store:
- selected model
- route params
- recreate id
- publication id
- prompt text for a single generator screen
- local dropdown state
- modal animation state
- upload previews
- temporary form controls

If the state belongs to the URL, Router owns it.
If it belongs to one component/flow, React owns it.
If it needs to be shared across the application and is client-only, Zustand owns it.

### 4. Local UI state → React

Use normal React state for:

- prompt editing
- open/closed local dropdowns
- model-picker UI state before navigation
- local tabs that are not navigation
- upload previews
- temporary settings
- textarea/input values
- local dialog internals

Avoid unnecessary global state.

## Auth / gating architecture

The auth/access behavior should be centralized.

Observed product behavior:

Unauthenticated Generate
→ Auth modal

Authenticated Generate without required access/credits
→ Upgrade modal

Use Zustand for the mock authenticated session so signing in from one place updates the entire application.

Example conceptually:

`useAppStore(state => state.user)`

Do not implement Clerk.

Do not implement real subscriptions.

A mock successful sign-in should update Zustand and allow the evaluator to immediately see the authenticated version of Library/header/gating behavior.

## Likes

Seeded generation data remains immutable source data.

Authenticated Like interactions may use Zustand to store lightweight overrides, e.g.:

`likedGenerationIds`

Do not mutate imported mock data.

Logged out:
Like
→ Auth modal
→ no state change

Logged in:
Like
→ toggle Zustand state
→ `aria-pressed` updates
→ count updates visually

Do not create a Favorites product.

## Recreate

Recreate data should NOT live in Zustand.

Generation records contain the seeded source data.

Recreate:

generation record
→ resolve destination/model/settings
→ navigate with TanStack Router
→ destination shell reads URL
→ `recreateId` resolves seeded generation
→ initialize generator state

This keeps Recreate deep-linkable and avoids hidden global navigation state.

## Generator state

Image and Video share primitives but keep their local form state within their respective features.

Do not create a universal Zustand generator store.

A generator page should derive:

- model from Router
- recreated source from seeded data
- editable prompt/settings from local React state

Use effects only where genuinely required to initialize/clean up external state.

## Styling

Continue following:

- verified semantic theme variables
- Tailwind mapped to those variables
- Inter for UI
- Space Grotesk for verified accent/headline usage

Do not copy Higgsfield production CSS.

## i18n

Continue using the small English semantic JSON + `t()` helper.

Do not reproduce Higgsfield's Lingui runtime.

## Architecture rule

Before introducing state, ask:

1. Is it navigational/product identity? → Router
2. Is it remote/server data? → Query
3. Must unrelated components/routes share it? → Zustand
4. Otherwise → local React state

Do not blur these responsibilities.

Update `.cursor/rules/react-project.mdc` with these decisions.

Do not start application implementation yet.

At the end tell me only:
1. what architecture rules changed
2. what Zustand will own
3. what Router will own
4. what remains local React state
5. whether any dependency needs to be installed once implementation begins

1. **Architecture rules changed**  
   `.cursor/rules/react-project.mdc` is now the project’s always-on rebuild rule. It locks Vite + React + TypeScript + TanStack Router + Tailwind + optional Radix + small Zustand; bans Next.js, Clerk, real billing, Query-for-mocks, Higgsfield CSS/Lingui, and a global generator store. State must be classified before it is added (Router → Query → Zustand → React).

2. **Zustand will own**  
   Mock auth/session, authenticated Like overrides (`likedGenerationIds` + visual counts), optional billing-cycle preference, and only other genuinely cross-route demo state.

3. **Router will own**  
   Product identity in the URL: model, `recreateId`, publication id, effect slug, meaningful generator mode, and tabs that are navigation. Recreate is navigate-then-hydrate from seeded data, not hidden global state.

4. **Local React state remains**  
   Prompt/settings, dropdowns, pre-navigation picker UI, non-nav tabs, upload previews, inputs, and dialog internals. Image and Video keep their own form state.

5. **Install once implementation begins**  
   `react`, `react-dom`, TypeScript, Vite, TanStack Router, Tailwind, Zustand, and Radix only where a primitive is needed. Do **not** install Next.js, Clerk, Lingui, or TanStack Query until there is real async/server state.

## Shared modal architecture

There are two major shared modal systems in the product and they must be implemented as reusable application-level components.

### 1. Auth modal

Use one shared `AuthModal`.

It is opened from multiple places:

- Generate while logged out
- Like while logged out
- Recreate when auth is required
- Header Login
- Header Sign up
- Library/Profile sign-in CTA

The modal may switch internal mode between:

- Welcome / auth entry
- Sign in
- Sign up
- email continuation

Do NOT create:
- `ImageLoginModal`
- `VideoLoginModal`
- `LikeLoginModal`
- separate auth dialogs per page

All auth-triggering actions should call the same shared modal system.

Recommended ownership:

- open/close + requested auth intent may live in shared UI state if multiple unrelated routes need to trigger it
- authenticated user/session lives in Zustand
- modal-specific form inputs stay local inside `AuthModal`

The modal should visually match the observed Higgsfield auth modal.

No Clerk integration.

---

### 2. Upgrade / pricing modal

Use one shared `UpgradeModal`.

It is opened from multiple gated actions, including:

- Image Generate while authenticated but gated
- Video Generate while authenticated but gated
- premium model access
- insufficient credits
- Pricing header action if we decide to reuse the modal there

Do NOT create:
- `ImagePricingModal`
- `VideoPricingModal`
- `SeedancePricingModal`
- separate copies of pricing plan cards

The shared modal has:

- contextual heading
- contextual description
- Monthly / Annual toggle
- Basic plan
- Pro plan
- Max plan
- Teams plan
- shared feature/pricing body

Only the reason/context changes.

Examples:

Image/model gate:

Title:
`Unlock GPT Image 2.0`

Description:
`Get access to GPT Image 2.0 image generations with a Starter or higher-tier plan`

Video/credit gate:

Title:
`Upgrade Plan to buy credits`

Description:
`Choose a higher plan for increased limits and credits top-up`

Model the modal API around a gating reason/context rather than around the page that opened it.

For example conceptually:

`openUpgradeModal({ reason: "model-access", modelId: "gpt_image_2" })`

or

`openUpgradeModal({ reason: "credits" })`

The pricing body remains shared.

Billing-cycle state may live in Zustand if we want the Monthly/Annual selection to persist across openings.

Do not implement checkout or payment processing.

---

## Modal rule

The application should have:

- one Auth modal system
- one Upgrade/Pricing modal system

Feature components trigger these shared systems.

Feature components must not own duplicated modal implementations.

- `data-loaded-src`

If Higgsfield's Cloudflare image proxy wraps an original URL, recover the underlying source URL where possible.

Download only assets needed for our Header / mega menus.

## If normal hover does not work

Try these fallbacks in order:

### A. Browser automation hover

Use the browser/agent interaction capability to hover the trigger rather than clicking the URL.

### B. Keyboard interaction

Focus the menu trigger and use the keyboard interaction supported by the live component to open it.

### C. Inspect open state

If necessary, use browser DevTools/runtime inspection to dispatch the appropriate pointer/mouse event to the actual trigger and then inspect the resulting DOM.

Do NOT fake the menu by manually changing `data-state="closed"` to `"open"` unless that genuinely causes the application to mount the content. Radix state attributes alone may only reflect state and may not control it.

### D. Network fallback

While opening the menu, watch newly requested image/SVG assets in the network log.

Map those assets back to their visible labels.

If an asset still cannot be resolved, mark it UNKNOWN rather than approximating it.

## Radix-specific note

The current Header uses Radix-style triggers such as:

- `data-nav-trigger`
- `aria-controls`
- `data-state="closed"`

The actual open menu may be rendered in a separate NavigationMenu viewport/portal rather than directly beneath the trigger.

Search the live DOM after opening for the matching `aria-controls` id and for newly mounted Radix content.

`StatusBadge` is the shared pill, with three color variants:

- **lime** — NEW (`#d1fe17`, dark text, lime well border)
- **pink** — TOP (`#ff005b`, white text, magenta well border)
- **cyan** — Seedance TOP (gradient pill, `#3CD8FF` well border)

It sits on the icon well (`absolute -top-3`, skewed Grotesk uppercase), not next to the title. `new` maps to lime, `top` maps to pink; Seedance sets `badgeColor: "cyan"`.

Open Image or Video in the header and you should see TOP/NEW on the wells.

Before we continue implementation, fix our research workflow so you do NOT repeatedly scan the HAR for styling details.

We already completed broad research and theme extraction. The HAR should now be treated as a frozen raw source, not something you repeatedly parse during normal implementation.

Do ONE final style-normalization pass.

Use:
- the existing HAR
- captured Higgsfield HTML we already have
- extracted theme files
- existing research files
- existing Header / mega-menu HTML references

The purpose is to create a compact, searchable implementation reference containing the actual Higgsfield CSS rules we are likely to need.

Extract and normalize the relevant styles for:

1. Header
2. Header actions
3. scrollable nav
4. badges
5. mega menus
6. Home section layout
7. gallery/grid layout
8. Generation card
9. Preset / Effects card
10. Project card
11. card media
12. creator/avatar row
13. Like control
14. Recreate overlay
15. View All / section actions
16. hover, active and open states for the above
17. relevant responsive rules

Do not copy entire CSS bundles.

Instead, find the live classes/selectors used by the captured HTML and extract only the rules that affect those components, including relevant media queries and CSS variables.

Create:

`research/reference/styles/header.reference.css`
`research/reference/styles/mega-menu.reference.css`
`research/reference/styles/home-gallery.reference.css`
`research/reference/styles/cards.reference.css`

Also create:

`research/COMPONENT_STYLE_REFERENCE.md`

For every important component record the resolved values we care about, for example:

- dimensions
- padding
- gap
- radius
- font size / weight / line height
- color/token
- background
- border
- shadow
- opacity
- blur
- hover
- active/open state
- responsive differences

Clearly mark values as:

- VERIFIED FROM LIVE CSS
- VERIFIED FROM COMPUTED STYLE
- APPROXIMATION

Where the live class references CSS variables, resolve the variable using our existing theme reference rather than making Cursor look it up later.

For the Home gallery specifically, create one reusable reference for each of the three card families:

- GenerationCard
- PresetCard
- ProjectCard

Do not create section-specific CSS for GPT Image, Soul, Seedance, etc. unless the live site genuinely differs.

The goal is that after this pass I can ask:

“make the GenerationCard like Higgsfield”

and you can implement it entirely from our research files WITHOUT reopening or reparsing the HAR.

After creating these references, add this rule to `.cursor/rules/react-project.mdc`:

- current responsive widths and virtualization strategy

Make only the following quality fixes.

1. VIDEO PLAYBACK

The current FeaturedCard starts/stops video only from `onPointerEnter` / `onPointerLeave`.

Replace hover-only playback with visibility-based playback suitable for desktop and mobile.

Because the horizontal list is already virtualized, only a small number of cards are mounted, so keep this simple.

Videos should:
- use `preload="none"`
- remain muted
- loop
- use `playsInline`
- use poster when one exists
- play when sufficiently visible inside the featured slider viewport
- pause when leaving visibility
- respect `prefers-reduced-motion`

Do not reset `currentTime` every time a card briefly leaves visibility unless there is a good reason.

Avoid creating an elaborate global video manager.

Prefer one clean IntersectionObserver rooted to the slider viewport, or another small reusable solution that works on both desktop and mobile.

2. LOAD-MORE DEDUPLICATION

The virtualizer `onChange` can run repeatedly while the final rendered item remains inside the load threshold.

Ensure `onEndReached` fires only once for the current item count/page.

Do not allow multiple rapid calls to append several pages because the virtualizer emitted several changes before React committed the new item count.

**Video playback.** One `IntersectionObserver` is rooted on the featured scroller. Mounted cards play when about half-visible, pause when they leave, stay muted/`playsInline`/`preload="none"`, and skip playback when `prefers-reduced-motion` is on. Pause no longer resets `currentTime`.

**Load-more.** `onEndReached` is keyed to the current `items.length`, and `loadMore` ignores a second call for that same count, so the virtualizer cannot append several pages before React commits.

**External links.** `FeaturedSlide` has `external?: boolean`. The API card is marked `external: true` and opens with `target="_blank"` / `rel="noopener noreferrer"`.

## 1. Virtual padding and the real scroll edges

The virtualizer currently has:

```ts
paddingStart: 16
paddingEnd: 16

[LOG_ENTRY type=RESPONSE num=45 session=e43d6375]
timestamp: 2026-09-17T00:46:31.291Z
model: cursor-grok-4.6-high-fast

Arrow targeting was following the virtualizer’s padded offsets and a stale card index, not the scroller’s real `0 … max` range.

**Padding vs edges.** Item 0 starts at 16px, so Previous landed on `16` instead of `0`. That left a sliver of scroll, kept the Previous arrow visible, and made another Previous click a no-op. Index `0` now scrolls to **`0`**; the last card scrolls to the real max. `scrollPaddingStart/End` is `16` so middle cards stay aligned with that inset.

**Index vs live scroll.** Repeated Next could retarget the same card while a smooth scroll was in flight, or after a partial snap. Clicks now advance a committed index during animation, then resync from `scrollLeft` when the scroll finishes or you scrub by hand. A leftover `16px` Previous click goes to `0` and the arrow hides.

Next was stopping after one click because the scroller parks **16px before** the next card (`item.start − padding`). After the animation ended, that position was read back as the **previous** card, so the next click aimed at the same offset and bailed out.

The arrow index now uses the same padded offsets it scrolls to, so each Next/Previous click advances to the actual next card.

A few small cleanup points on the FeaturedSlider:

1. Right now `scrollLeft` is stored in React state and updates on every scroll event. That means the whole slider rerenders continuously while scrolling. If the slider still feels perfectly smooth, don’t refactor this yet. Just be aware of it and avoid adding more expensive work tied to every scroll event.

2. Change `SCROLL_EDGE_PX` from `1` to around `3`. We already had an issue where the slider was visually at the beginning but still had a tiny fractional scroll offset, so the previous arrow stayed visible. A slightly larger tolerance is safer.

3. The card width estimate changes at the responsive breakpoints, but the virtualizer may not automatically remeasure if the browser is resized across those breakpoints after the component has already mounted. Add a lightweight resize/re-measure solution so TanStack Virtual recalculates correctly when the slider/container width changes. Keep this simple, ideally using ResizeObserver or an existing virtualizer remeasure mechanism.

4. Leave the duplicated desktop/mobile anchors alone for now. Some cards genuinely have different mobile and desktop URLs, and this also matches the Higgsfield markup closely. Don’t introduce a JS media-query hook just to remove those two anchors.

Do not redesign or restructure the slider. These are only small stability/performance cleanups.

[LOG_ENTRY type=RESPONSE num=47 session=e43d6375]
timestamp: 2026-09-17T00:55:28.987Z
model: cursor-grok-4.6-high-fast

`scrollLeft` is unchanged, so scrolling still updates React state; nothing extra was added on that path.

`SCROLL_EDGE_PX` is now **3**, so a leftover subpixel offset at the start won’t keep the Previous arrow visible.

A `ResizeObserver` on the scroller calls `virtualizer.measure()` (coalesced to one animation frame) when the container size changes, so card widths remeasure across the `md` / `xl` breakpoints after mount.

Desktop/mobile hit-area anchors were left as they are.

Yeah, this version is much better. I’d keep the virtualization architecture now. The scroll logic is much more coherent than the previous version.

The important improvement is that Cursor now separates the logical navigation index from the physical smooth scroll. While an animation is running, repeated clicks advance indexRef rather than recalculating from the halfway-scrolled DOM position, which is what was causing your skipped/ignored clicks before. It also special-cases index 0 to offset 0, so the 16px virtual padding no longer traps the rail slightly away from the real start.

The virtualization itself looks clean: native overflow-x-scroll, TanStack only controlling which items exist, fixed responsive estimates, real measurement, overscan of 2, and absolute positioning from the virtualizer. That's a reasonable implementation for a feed containing dozens of video cards.

The video strategy is also much simpler now. FeaturedSlider determines whether each mounted virtual card is at least 50% visible and passes that down as inView; the hook plays or pauses accordingly and respects reduced motion. That's cleaner than having an IntersectionObserver system layered on top of a virtualizer that already knows every card's geometry.

There are four things I would still clean up, but none require redesigning the slider.

scrollLeft is React state and updates on every scroll event. That means the whole FeaturedSlider rerenders continuously during smooth scrolling so it can recalculate video visibility and arrow state. With only a handful of virtual items mounted this isn't catastrophic, but it is the main performance thing I'd watch. If scrolling feels perfectly smooth, don't touch it right now. For a take-home, working/fidelity beats another refactor.
SCROLL_EDGE_PX = 1 is a little too strict. You previously had exactly the fractional-scroll problem. I'd use something like 2–3px for arrow edge detection. Browser scrolling can end at fractional CSS pixels depending on DPR. This is tiny but worthwhile.
Responsive resizing across breakpoints isn't fully handled. estimateCardWidth() correctly returns 312 / 400 / 512, matching your live card widths, but the virtualizer isn't explicitly told to remeasure if the browser crosses one of those breakpoints after mounting. Normal users won't constantly resize the site, so I wouldn't block progress on it, but a ResizeObserver/remeasure would make it robust later.
There is still duplicated desktop/mobile anchor markup for cards with different destinations. It's not spaghetti, and it's actually very close to Higgsfield's captured HTML, so given this is a recreation assignment I'd leave it rather than introducing a responsive-JS hook just to eliminate two tiny anchors.

One thing I specifically would not change now is useFeaturedFeed. It has a simple interface, page growth is isolated, and it has protection against requesting the same count twice. The slider has its own corresponding guard too, so the infinite behavior shouldn't repeatedly append pages from one virtualizer update.

The data layer is also in decent shape. You now have the large real Higgsfield catalog with explicit CDN media, mobile/desktop destinations, and external-link metadata rather than invented cards.

What I'd do now

I wouldn't refactor it further before continuing Home.

I'd make only this tiny change:

const SCROLL_EDGE_PX = 3;

Then visually test:

next next next next quickly
prev prev prev prev quickly
manual scroll halfway → next
manual scroll halfway → prev
scroll far right → repeated prev back to zero
resize once desktop → smaller desktop

If those feel clean, commit it.

Arrow navigation maintains a logical target independently from the in-progress smooth scroll, which also keeps rapid clicks predictable.”

That explains why the code exists, rather than just saying you used TanStack Virtual.

With only a handful of virtual items mounted this isn't catastrophic, but it is the main performance thing I'd watch. If scrolling feels perfectly smooth, don't touch it right now. Responsive resizing across breakpoints isn't fully handled. estimateCardWidth() correctly returns 312 / 400 / 512, matching your live card widths, but the virtualizer isn't explicitly told to remeasure if the browser crosses one of those breakpoints after mounting. Normal users won't constantly resize the site, so I wouldn't block progress on it, but a ResizeObserver/remeasure would make it robust later.

Keep card content/assets in `quick-start.data.ts`.

Do not scatter external asset URLs throughout JSX.

Use small typed data such as:

```ts
type QuickStartItem = {
  id: string
  title: string
  description: string
  href: string
  icon: ...
  badge?: "top" | "new"
  type?: "Video" | "Image"
  desktopOnly?: boolean
}

Adjust the type to match the actual supplied content rather than forcing unnecessary fields.

IMPORTANT

Do not build the GPT Astra banner below this section yet.

Do not modify:

Header
FeaturedSlider
routing architecture
theme foundation
global button primitives unless an actual bug requires it

Do not use HAR to search unrelated things.

After implementation:

run TypeScript
run ESLint
fix errors
stop

I will visually test the section manually.


That keeps Cursor focused instead of letting it disappear into another full-site HAR investigation.

Visual Effects is the first Home gallery, built as a reusable section shell rather than a one-off row.

`HomeGallery` is the shared chrome: title, subtitle, optional header action, optional clipped “View all” fade. Visual Effects supplies the masonry cards; later galleries (Genjutsu, Seedance, GPT Image, and so on) can reuse the same shell with different children.

```tsx
<HomeGallery
  title="Visual Effects"
  titleHref="/effects"
  description="Big-budget visual effects, from explosions to surreal transformations."
  action={{ href: "/effects/use", label: "Start generating" }}
  viewAllHref="/effects"
>
  {/* section-specific cards */}
</HomeGallery>
```

**Layout**
- `src/features/home/gallery/` — `HomeGallery`, `GalleryHeader`, `GalleryRail`, types
- `src/features/home/effects/` — `VisualEffectsSection`, preset data, `EffectPresetCard`

Visual Effects is a 5-column masonry clip (`max-h-224` + page-color fade), not a horizontal rail. `GalleryRail` is there for later strip galleries. Recreate stays a link to `/effects/use/{slug}`; the card itself goes to `/effects/examples/{slug}`.

The section is desktop-only (`hidden md:block`), matching live Home. It sits under the Astra banner. `/effects` pages are not built yet, so those links will 404 until we add them.

The aspect ratios are correct and should stay. They match Higgsfield's actual rendered media dimensions.

However, do not store Visual Effects as precomputed `visualEffectColumns[][]`.

The live Higgsfield DOM shows a JS-computed masonry layout:
- parent is `position: relative`
- list items are `position: absolute`
- each item gets calculated `top`, `left`, `width`
- inner height comes from its media aspect ratio
- observed desktop card width is 360px
- observed gap is 8px
- column positions are 0, 368, 736, 1104, 1472 at this viewport

Keep the content as one flat ordered array.

Then make the masonry layout calculate the number of columns from available container width and place each item into the currently shortest column.

Use:

height = columnWidth / aspectRatio

and an 8px horizontal/vertical gap.

Do not hardcode the captured desktop columns into the data because column count changes responsively.

Before changing it, inspect the relevant HAR/JS bundle specifically for this masonry list and determine whether Higgsfield uses an existing masonry/virtualization library or a custom layout algorithm.

If an identifiable library is used, report it first.

If the exact library cannot be verified, implement the observed behavior using a small reusable masonry layout, but do not change the verified aspect ratios or item order. and dont test it again, ill test it manually, also you extracted webp only, these are vids so pls get the vid cdns too

timestamp: 2026-09-17T01:49:10.558Z
model: cursor-grok-4.6-high-fast

Higgsfield’s Visual Effects list is **masonic**, not CSS columns. The bundle is minified so the package name is gone, but the API is the same: `overscanBy: 2`, `columnGutter` / `rowGutter`, and `shortestColumn: () => heights.length > 1 ? Math.min.apply(null, heights) : heights[0] || 0`. Their wrapper feeds it `columnWidth: 160`, `8px` gutters, and `maxColumnCount` default `5`. At the viewport you measured, that produces 360px cards at `0, 368, 736, 1104, 1472`.

I did not add the masonic package. Fifteen items do not need its window virtualizer, so `GalleryMasonry` implements that same shortest-column packer and sizes columns from container width.

Content is a single ordered array again (`visualEffects`), source order preserved, aspect ratios unchanged. Each preset now has the HAR `preview_url` mp4 plus the webp poster. Videos play muted while in view.

The proposed composition model is the right direction, but the mapping was too flat. Live Home is **four families**, not three layouts plus four cards.

## Corrected architecture

Keep composition. Do **not** build one mega-`Gallery` with variant booleans, and do **not** put Genjutsu or Projects on `ColumnGallery`.

| Family | Live runtime | Rebuild |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Viral hub | Masonic positioner (`overscanBy: 2`, `columnWidth: 160`, gutters 8, max 5) | `MasonryGallery` + `EffectPresetCard` |
| Community | One `G` shell + `Kr` column grid + `z0` publication card | `HomeGallery` + `ColumnGallery` + `GenerationCard` |
| Genjutsu | Own section shell, own packer, own preset card / modal | Bespoke section + `PresetCard` |
| Projects | Own CSS auto-fit grid + `data-higgsfield-projects-card` | `GridGallery` + `ProjectCard` (own clip/fade) |

`GalleryRail` is unused here. None of these Home rows is a horizontal strip.

Cards stay with their features until a second section actually shares one. `GenerationCard` will be shared; Genjutsu/Effects/Projects cards will not.

`IMPLEMENTATION_SCOPE.md` still drops **Marketing Studio** and **Seedance 2.0** Home rows even though live uses the same `G` component.

---

## 1. Community gallery family — confirmed, one implementation

Seedance 2.5, GPT Image 2, Marketing Studio, Seedance 2.0, Soul Cinema, and Soul 2.0 all render through the same `G` → `Kr` → publication card path:

- `maxCols: 4`
- `baseHref: publications`
- `links: []` (no chip row / no header Start generating)
- clip `h-224` + fade `h-52` + CTA `View all of {name}` → `/{slug}`
- card: shared `z0` (`media` + `profile` + Like). Home does **not** pass a Recreate footer.

Seedance 2.5 is the only row with a presentation extra: `origin-bottom scale-125` on img/video. That is a figure class, not a second layout.

### How items become columns (not guessed)

The API/loader returns a **flat list**. Each item is `{ id, preview, publication }`. Columns are **not** pre-grouped on the server.

`Kr` packs on the client with size `preview.height / preview.width` (relative height if columns share width):

1. **First pack / item reset / append:** shortest-column (`indexOf(Math.min(...heights))`, first column wins ties).
2. **Resize when column count changes:**
   - shrink: keep the first N columns, flatten the overflow columns, **round-robin** leftovers onto those N
   - grow: **round-robin the original flat list** (not a fresh shortest-column pack)
3. **Column count** comes from `window.innerWidth`, not container width: for `maxCols: 4` → 4 / 3 / 2 / 2 at >1280 / ≤1280 / ≤1024 / ≤768.
4. **Paint:** CSS grid of **column stacks** (`grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4`), each stack `space-y-2` with index-based `pr-2`. It is **not** a flat CSS grid of cards. `home-generation-grid` in the style reference is APPROX and wrong.

SSR Seedance HTML with four placeholder stacks is the **empty shimmer**, not server-packed real cards. Hydration fills `Kr` from the flat list.

`ColumnGallery` therefore: flat items in → shortest-column by `1 / aspectRatio` → grid of stacks. For this rebuild it **re-packs on column-count change** instead of copying the live shrink/grow hybrid (that hybrid exists for infinite-scroll reuse and makes order jump).

No virtualization on these rows. `inView` (`triggerOnce`) only swaps shimmer vs cards. Masonic is **not** used here.

---

## 2. Aspect ratios — media metadata

They belong on the item. Your `GenerationItem` shape is correct.

Live `aspect-ratio` is `preview.width / preview.height`. Packing uses the inverse. RSC examples:

| Pixels | CSS ratio | What it is |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1080×1920 | **0.5625** | 9:16 video |
| 1520×2688 | **0.565476** | GPT Image 2 pixel size, not a rounded 9:16 |
| 1744×2336 | **0.746575** | near 3:4, still exact pixels |
| 1440×1080 | **1.33333** | 4:3 |
| 1920×1080 / 3840×2160 | **1.77778** | 16:9 |

Hardcoded 0.5625 / 1.333 / 1.777 on SSR tiles are **shimmer placeholders**, not per-job data.

Exceptions (do not put raw pixel ratio on these):

- **Genjutsu:** buckets `"16/9" | "9/16" | "3/2"` with packing weights `9/16`, `16/9`, `2/3`
- **Projects:** fixed `aspect-343/195` on every Home card

[LOG_ENTRY type=RESPONSE num=58 session=e43d6375]
timestamp: 2026-09-17T03:25:29.932Z
model: cursor-grok-4.6-high-fast

That’s the **playback progress** for the lime ring around the selected circular thumbnail. It is supposed to keep changing.

While the preset video plays, `timeupdate` writes a 0–1 value onto the card footer:

```97:104:src/features/home/genjutsu/GenjutsuPresetCard.tsx
  const onTimeUpdate = useCallback(
    (event: SyntheticEvent<HTMLVideoElement>) => {
      const { currentTime, duration } = event.currentTarget;
      actionsRef.current?.style.setProperty(
        "--explore-video-progress",
        String(playbackProgress(currentTime, duration)),
      );
```

`0.974` means the clip is about 97% through. The selected thumb’s SVG ring reads that CSS variable to draw the arc:

```53:62:src/features/home/genjutsu/GenjutsuMediaThumb.tsx
          <circle
            cx="16"
            cy="16"
            r={GENJUTSU_PROGRESS_RADIUS}
            fill="none"
            stroke="var(--brand)"
            strokeWidth="2"
            strokeLinecap="round"
            strokeDasharray={GENJUTSU_PROGRESS_CIRCUMFERENCE}
            strokeDashoffset={`calc(${GENJUTSU_PROGRESS_CIRCUMFERENCE}px * (1 - var(--explore-video-progress, 0)))`}
```

It loops because the video is `loop`ed: progress goes `0 → 1`, then jumps back and repeats. Switching a thumbnail clears the variable and starts over. This is the same mechanism as the live Genjutsu cards, not a render bug.

The Explore more title was using the **wrong type scale**.

Theme discovery recorded `--hf-type-size-*` and the **lime gallery titles** (26px / 34px Grotesk). Live Explore more does not use that. It uses the separate `--text-brand-h-*` recipes:

- `text-brand-h-sm` → **1.75rem / 2.25rem / -2%**
- `md:text-brand-h-lg` → **3rem / 3.5rem / -2%**
- `font-bold` still wins for weight (700)

Those heading recipes were never in `tokens.dark.json`, so the section was approximated as `text-[1.625rem] md:text-[2.125rem]` (26px / 34px, default leading). Desktop was 34px instead of 48px.

They are now in the theme as `text-brand-h-*`. Explore more is `text-brand-h-sm md:text-brand-h-lg`. The footer heading uses `text-brand-h-xs md:text-brand-h-md` the same way.

[LOG_ENTRY type=RESPONSE num=80 session=e43d6375]
timestamp: 2026-09-17T05:04:10.188Z
model: cursor-grok-4.6-high-fast

Use **`useT()` for copy**, and **`useLocale()` only for the current language id**.

They are two different jobs:

- `useLocale()` — which language is selected (the language menu already uses this)
- `useT()` — subscribe to that locale, then translate. Internally it calls `useLocale()`, so the component re-renders when the language changes

There is no Lingui-style `useTranslation`. The API is:

```tsx
const t = useT();
t("home.exploreMore.title");
```

`t()` is now typed from `en.json`. Dotted keys autocomplete, and `t("home.exploreMore.titless")` is a TypeScript error.

LanguageMenu keeps `useLocale()` because it needs the locale id, not just translated strings. Catalogs are still English-only, so switching language re-renders but still shows English until more locales exist.

**Home review (no UI changes).** This ES HAR is a **partial capture**: 122 requests, empty `pages`, **no HTML document**. I could compare catalogs, CMS/API payloads, and media URLs — not layout, spacing, or CSS.

### Copy that will now match live `/es` (via `t()`)
Gallery headings, Genjutsu chrome, banner kickers/CTAs, Explore more title (`Explora más funciones de IA`), landing-footer heading, site-footer legal links, auth modal, likes/recreate.

### Copy that stays English on our Home
These are hardcoded in data files, so Español still shows English:

| Surface | Live `/es` | Ours |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Featured cards | CMS `cards?lang=es` — Spanish titles/descriptions | `featured-slider.data.ts` English |
| Quick Start + promo hero | not in this HAR as localized JSON | hardcoded English (`Sign up and get your extra discount`, etc.) |
| Visual Effects card titles | `Caída flotante`, `Deslizamiento de estudio`, … (45/50 names translated) | English slugs (`Floating fall`, …) |
| Genjutsu preset titles | 14/20 translated (`Dúo campestre`, `Chaqueta naranja`); 6 stay English (`Couple Recast`, …) | all English in `genjutsu.data.ts` |
| Explore more chips | several translated (`Efectos visuales`, `Comunidad`, `Editar imagen`, `Comercial`) | English labels in `explore-more.data.ts` |
| Header mega-menu **items** | translated in catalog | only column titles (`Funciones` / `Modelos` / `Complementos`) go through `t()`; row labels/descriptions stay English |
| Landing footer columns | would be translated | `Create`, `AI Video`, social names, etc. stay English |

### Content / data mismatches
1. **Featured set is different.** Live ES requested `size=5` and returned: Motion Designer, **Effects 2.0**, Genjutsu, GPT Image 2.5 Sunburst, HIGGSFIELD × GPT-6 ASTRA. Ours starts with **Higgsfield API**, then Genjutsu, Motion Designer, **Higgsfield Effects** (no “2.0”), then a long English catalog. Overlapping videos match (`8b8270cd…`, `31293efb…`, `16eebc9a…`); order and titles do not.
2. **Supercomputer art is locale-specific.** Live loads `static.higgsfield.ai/es/spc-banner/spc-banner-{marketing,creative,production}.png`. Ours always uses the unprefixed `/spc-banner/` assets.
3. **Projects** — same 8 films, English titles in both (film names). Fine.
4. **`home-trailer-un`** is still unimplemented on our page. Explore more is the last section.

### Capture limits
No `/es` HTML, so I did not re-check section order, masks, masonry, or desktop-only wrappers from this file. Those still rest on the earlier English Home work. This dump also fetched **English then Spanish** viral-hub and Genjutsu APIs — live may flash English data before `lang=es` returns.

### Catalog oddities (live, not us)
- Badge **Top** → **Arriba** (that is their string).
- **30% OFF** has no literal key; I used their `% DE DESCUENTO` pattern → `30% DE DESCUENTO`.
- Slider aria labels had no “Show next/previous”; closest live keys are “Mostrar la siguiente/anterior diapositiva”.

Sleep well. When you’re back, the highest-impact follow-ups are: localize the **data** (featured, VE titles, Genjutsu titles, explore-more chips, footer columns, mega-menu rows) and point Supercomputer images at `/es/spc-banner/` when locale is `es`.

## Collapsed architecture

**Models (do not spawn pages)**

- Image Models → **ImageShell** `/ai/image?model=…`  
  *(except Topaz → Upscale)*
- Video Models → **VideoShell** `/ai/video?model=…`  
  *(except Kling Motion → `/ai/video/motion`; Kling Omni Edit → `/ai/video/edit?model=…`)*
- Audio Models → **AudioShell TTS** `/audio?voiceMode=voiceover&audioModel=…`

**Same shell + mode / query / modal**

- Photodump → ImageShell + modal (`soul-v2`)
- Create Image / header Image → ImageShell, different default `model`
- Edit Video / Omni Edit → VideoShell Edit
- Motion Control → VideoShell Motion
- Reframe → VideoShell Reframe
- Draw to Video / Draw to Edit → VideoShell + inpaint overlay
- Voice Change / Translate → AudioShell tabs (`voiceMode`)
- UGC Factory → Lipsync + `ugc-studio`

**Other shells / apps / workspaces**

- Cinematic Cameras / Cinema Studio / header Cinema → **Cinema** `/generate`
- Canvas → **Canvas**
- Soul Moodboard → **Moodboard**
- Soul ID Character → **Character**
- AI Influencer → **Influencer**
- Relight (image) / Face Swap / Character Swap / Color Palette / Video Relight → **Apps**
- Inpaint / header Edit → **Layers**
- Image Upscale / Video Upscale / Topaz → **Upscale**
- Faceless / Shorts / Explainer / Mixed Media → **own studios**
- 3D Jutsu → **3D Jutsu**
- Click to Ad → **Marketing Studio**
- Lipsync Studio → **Lipsync**

```html

  <span class="absolute -left-0.5 -right-0.5 top-1/2 border-t-[0.09375rem] border-current rotate-30"></span>
</span>
<span class="">6.5</span>
```

Soul / Sunburst only render a single current amount (`0.125` / `3`). There is **no** evidence in these captures that quantity, aspect, quality, or resolution changes that number.

## 3. Proposed normalized config

The repo has **no** `ImageModelConfig` / `GenerateButton` yet. Header `MegaItem` is the wrong shape (href + mega-menu badge `new`/`top`, no controls/pricing). Use a dedicated image-engine config, keyed by **router** `model` (URL is source of truth). Keep `engineId` when HTML `data-model` differs.

```ts
type ImageModelId = string // URL query, e.g. "gpt_image_2" | "soul-v2" | "soul-cinematic"

type ImagePickerEntry =
  | {
      kind: "model"
      modelId: ImageModelId
      label: string
      description?: string
      badge?: "Premium" | "New"
      icon: ImageIconId
    }
  | {
      kind: "destination"
      href: string // e.g. "/apps/face-swap"
      label: string
      description?: string
      badge?: "Premium" | "New"
      icon: ImageIconId
    }

type ImageControlConfig =
  | { type: "aspect-ratio"; defaultValue: string }          // "3:4" | "16:9" | "Auto"
  | { type: "quality"; defaultValue: string }               // "High"
  | { type: "resolution"; defaultValue: string }            // "2k" | "2K"
  | { type: "select"; id: string; defaultValue: string }    // unlabeled GPT Image 2 Auto
  | { type: "toggle"; id: string; defaultValue: boolean }   // unlabeled Soul On/Off
  | { type: "action"; id: "camera" | "color-transfer"; badge?: "New" }

type ImageModelConfig = {
  id: ImageModelId                 // router ?model=
  engineId: string                 // fieldset data-model (imagegen_2_0 vs gpt_image_2)
  label: string                    // chip text
  icon: ImageIconId

  picker?: {                       // only if this engine appears in the composer picker
    description?: string
    badge?: "Premium" | "New"
  }

  references: {
    enabled: boolean
    multiple: boolean
    accept: string[]               // [".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp"]
  }

  characterSlot?: { enabled: true } // Soul only; omit when absent

  controls: ImageControlConfig[]   // ordered; shell maps type → component

  quantity: { default: number; max: number } // observed 1 / 4

  pricing: {
    credits: number
    originalCredits?: number       // present only when a strike is proven
  }

  emptyState?: {
    titlePrefix: string            // "Start creating with"
    titleHighlight: string         // "Higgsfield Soul Cinema"
    body: string
    media: string[]
  }
}
```

**Do not** encode option lists (`16:9` vs `1:1`, quality enum, etc.) until a capture has `role="option"` (all listboxes here are closed; **0 options** in DOM).

**Do not** put Face Swap / Character Swap into `ImageModelConfig[]` as engines. They belong on the picker list as `kind: "destination"`.

GenerateButton API (no hardcoded 8.5 / 6.5 / 3 / 0.125):

- `pricing.originalCredits == null` → `Generate {credits}`
- `pricing.originalCredits != null` → strike `originalCredits`, show `credits`

No quantity or settings multiplier unless later captures prove it.

## 4. Truly model-dynamic vs shared ImageShell state

### Model-dynamic (config)

- Router id + `engineId` + chip label + icon
- Picker description / Premium|New|none (when the engine is in the picker)
- Reference `multiple` (Soul single vs GPT `multiple`)
- Presence of CHARACTER slot
- `controls[]` composition and **defaults** (aspect, quality, resolution, Camera, Color Transfer + New, unlabeled toggle)
- `pricing.credits` / optional `originalCredits`
- Empty-state title/media **only when the selected model actually owns them** (proven: Soul Cinema)

### Shared ImageShell (not per-model if/else)

- Route `/ai/image`, form `#image-form`, fieldset layout, blur/border chrome
- Lexical prompt, placeholder `Describe the scene you imagine`, `id="hf:tour-image-prompt"`
- Model chip + picker **trigger** (`data-tour-anchor="tour-image-model"` / `data-panel-toggle`)
- Quantity stepper chrome (`Decrement` / `1 /4` / `Increment`)
- Settings scroller (`Scroll settings left/right`)
- Reference **uploader chrome** + shared `accept` list (behavior flags come from config)
- Generate button chrome (`id="hf:image-form-submit"`); **numbers come from config**
- Local React state: prompt text, open picker, open Camera/Color Transfer panels, uploads, quantity, selected control values
- Router owns `?model=` (and later `recreateId`). Zustand does **not**.

The filled prompt `generate a guy working on his laptop…` is **session leftover**, identical on all four shells — not a model default.

- **List option catalogs** (aspect/quality/resolution/4th Auto) — menus closed, `role="option"` count = 0.
- **GPT Image 2’s fourth `Auto`** — unlabeled; do not name it background/output/style.
- **Soul On/Off** — unlabeled; do not call it “Elements” (that name is from Video research, not these captures). Why Cinema defaults On and Soul 2.0 Off is unknown beyond “different defaults.”
- **Camera dialog contents** and **Color Transfer panel contents**.
- **Character panel contents**; whether CHARACTER uses the duplicate `#image-form-reference` (duplicate identical ids on Soul).
- **Quantity max** — `/4` plus enabled Increment is the only evidence; increment-at-4 and whether Generate cost scales are unproven.
- **Empty states for GPT Image 2, Sunburst, Soul 2.0** — GPT/Sunburst dumps still contain Soul Cinema empty-state nodes; Soul 2.0 is a hydrated “General” + Change + style webp, so its true empty copy/media are unobserved.
- **Whether Soul 2.0 “General” + Change is default composer UI or recreate/style hydration** (prompt is also dirty).
- **Picker parent group** and how nested groups relate to the four selected engines.
- **Internal ids / URLs** for picker rows that do not uniquely match mega-menu Models (Nano Banana, Higgsfield Soul, GPT Image vs GPT Image 2 vs 1.5, Seedream 4.0, FLUX.2 Pro/Flex/Max, Flux Kontext Max, Multi Reference, Kling O1, WAN 2.2, Recraft V4.1 Utility).
- **Selected-row UI** in the picker (no `aria-pressed` / `data-model` on buttons).
- **Icon asset IDs** beyond “Soul family SVG vs GPT family SVG vs per-row picker SVGs” (Z-Image and WAN 2.2 share the same path prefix in this dump — treat as capture oddity, not a design rule).

## Implementation rule (when you say to build)

Drive the shell from `controls[]` + `references` + `pricing` + optional `characterSlot` / `emptyState`. Switching `?model=` swaps config. **No `if (model === "soul-v2")` chains.** Picker rows that are apps stay `kind: "destination"` and leave `/ai/image`.

Not implementing ImageShell until you want this config locked in and coded.

The rebuild currently has **one static document head**. There is no route `head()`, no Helmet, and no favicon. Live Image titles stay on the route when `?model=` changes.

## 1. Current metadata implementation in this repo

This is **Vite + TanStack Router (client SPA)**, not TanStack Start. Routes today: `__root__` and `/` only.

| Location | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `index.html` | **Only** metadata owner: `lang="en"`, viewport, `color-scheme=dark`, `theme-color=#030304`, `<title>Higgsfield</title>` |
| `src/main.tsx` | `createRouter({ routeTree })` — no head config |
| `src/routes/__root.tsx` | `createRootRoute` + `AppShell` + `Outlet`. **No `head`, no `<HeadContent />`** |
| `src/routes/index.tsx` | `createFileRoute("/")` — component only |
| `src/app/AppShell.tsx` | Chrome only |
| `public/` | Country flags only. **No** `favicon`, `icon.png`, `apple-icon.png`, `manifest.webmanifest` |
| `src/lib/i18n.ts` / locales | UI copy, **not** document titles |
| Dependencies | No `react-helmet`, `react-helmet-async`, `useHead` |

Repo search: no `document.title`, no icon/manifest links, no `head:` / `meta:` / `links:` on routes. `createRootRouteWithContext` is unused.

`@tanstack/react-router@1.170.37` **does** export `HeadContent`, `Asset`, and `MetaDescriptor`. That API is installed and unused.

So every future route will keep showing **Higgsfield** until something writes a title.

---

## 2. Global vs route vs model ownership

### Global (once, at app/root)

Should live in `index.html` + `public/` (and optionally a root `head()` for client navigations):

| Item | Live (confirmed) | Our repo now |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Favicon | `/icon.png` as `rel="icon"` and `rel="shortcut icon"` | missing |
| Apple touch | `/apple-icon.png` (`apple-touch-icon` + `apple-touch-icon-precomposed`) | missing |
| Manifest | `/manifest.webmanifest` | missing |
| `color-scheme` | `dark` | already in `index.html` |
| `theme-color` | HAR/Home research **`#030304`**; hydrated `/ai/image` **`#0f1113`** | `#030304` (matches Home research) |
| Fallback title | Live brand **Higgsfield**; Home SEO is longer (below) | `Higgsfield` |
| PWA `theme_color` | **`#D1FE17`** in the manifest (lime, not the meta theme-color) | n/a |

### Route-level

Live product sets a **per-path SEO title**. Same Image URL with different `?model=` does **not**.

### Query/model-level

`Create AI Images from Text & Photo | Higgsfield`

Icons, manifest, `color-scheme`, and `theme-color` were unchanged. Model stays under the Image route title. Do not key `document.title` off `model`.

Effects slugs are path params, not Image `?model=`. i18n has `{preset} AI Visual Effect Generator | Higgsfield` — **not live-checked**; do not assume Image-style stability for `/effects/use/:slug`.

## 5. Who should own what (reuse, no Helmet)

The stack already has TanStack Router document-head management. **Do not add React Helmet.**

| Concern | Owner | Why |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Favicon / apple icon files | `public/icon.png`, `public/apple-icon.png` (and PWA sizes only if we ship a manifest) | Vite serves `public/` at `/` |
| Global icon + theme + fallback title | **`index.html`** | First paint before JS; this is the current (and correct) static shell |
| Client title updates on navigation | **`createFileRoute(…).head`** + **`<HeadContent />` in `src/routes/__root.tsx`** | Native Router API; React 19 will hoist `<title>`/`<meta>` |
| Default/fallback after client nav | Root `createRootRoute({ head: … })` matching `index.html` | So unmatched/omitted routes still say Higgsfield |
| Static route titles (`/`, `/ai/image`, `/ai/video`, `/library/all`, …) | That route’s `head()`; strings in `src/locales` | i18n already owns copy |
| Publication title | `src/routes/publications/$id.tsx` `head` from loader/params (`username`) | Live template is username-based, not prompt-based |
| Model query | **Do not own title** | Proven on Image |

Suggested shape (conceptual, not implementing):

```ts
// each route
export const Route = createFileRoute("/ai/image")({
  head: () => ({
    meta: [{ title: t("meta.imageTitle") }],
  }),
  component: ImagePage,
})
```

Keep `?model=` out of `head`. For publications, `head: ({ loaderData }) => ({ meta: [{ title: … }] })`.

Optional later: a tiny `src/lib/meta.ts` helper so titles stay consistent (`{page} | Higgsfield` vs `• Higgsfield`). Not a new package.

PWA manifest: live exists, rebuild does not. Skip unless we explicitly want installability; it is not required for tab titles.

| `public/icon.png`, `public/apple-icon.png` | Add assets (no files there now) |
| `src/routes/__root.tsx` | Render `<HeadContent />`; optional root `head()` fallback |
| `src/routes/index.tsx` | Home `head` title |
| Future `src/routes/ai/image.tsx`, `ai/video.tsx`, `effects/…`, `community.tsx`, `library/all.tsx`, `publications/$id.tsx`, `$username/projects/$slug.tsx` | Per-route `head` |
| `src/locales/en.json` (+ `es.json`) | `meta.*` strings |
| `public/manifest.webmanifest` | Only if PWA is in scope |

The Soul Cinema empty state now matches the captured markup instead of the plain 4-up grid I had before. It was rendering as an even grid of rectangles; it now renders as the overlapping fanned card cluster with the blurred duplicate behind it.

What I built in `ImageShell.tsx`:

- A single `EmptyStateCardRow` rendered twice — once absolutely positioned at `top-0`, centered, with `blur-[32px] opacity-40` as the glow layer, and once normally as the sharp row.
- Per-card presentation taken from the capture, applied by slot index: `-rotate-10` bordered rounded square, `rotate-4` unbordered rounded square, `rotate-180 -scale-y-100` bordered circle, `-rotate-4` bordered rounded square.
- Captured metrics kept verbatim: `size-[clamp(64px,min(12vw,16vh),172px)]`, overlap `-mr-[clamp(16px,min(1.5vw,2vh),36px)]` on all but the last card, descending `z-index` 4→1, `border-3 xl:border-4 border-white/30`, and the two-layer shadow.
- Container spacing now uses `gap-[clamp(16px,3vh,40px)]` outside and `gap-[clamp(12px,2.5vh,32px)]` inside, with the text block as `gap-2`, and the content area reserves the captured 244px for the composer so the copy no longer sits under it.

One thing worth knowing for future styling work here: Tailwind v4 emits rotation as the standalone `rotate` CSS property, not inside `transform`. I initially read `transform: none` in the browser and thought the rotations had failed; they were applying the whole time. Verified computed values on the live page are `-10deg`, `4deg`, `180deg` with `scale: 1 -1`, and the third card's radius resolves to a full circle. Lint and `tsc` are clean.

I did not reuse this empty state for any other model — it's still only on `soul-cinematic`, and the two srcset/CDN wrappers from the captured DOM were left out since we load the `static.higgsfield.ai` originals directly.

timestamp: 2026-09-17T11:43:06.855Z
model: claude-opus-5-thinking-medium

Both are fixed.

**Explore nav:** the bug was in the `/ai/image` route, not the header. My canonicalization effect read `useRouterState`, which already points at the *next* location while a navigation is pending. Clicking Explore made the location `/` with no `model` param, the still-mounted `ImagePage` saw "no valid model," and replaced the URL straight back to `/ai/image`. Every link out of the shell was dead, not just Explore. The effect now only runs when the router's pathname is actually `/ai/image`:

```25:41:src/routes/ai/image.tsx
function ImagePage() {
  const { model } = Route.useSearch();
  const navigate = useNavigate({ from: "/ai/image" });
  const location = useRouterState({
    select: (state) => ({
      pathname: state.location.pathname,
      rawModel: new URLSearchParams(state.location.searchStr).get("model"),
    }),
  });

  // The router state already points at the next location while a navigation is
  // pending, so leaving /ai/image must not re-canonicalize the model.
  const needsCanonicalModel =
    location.pathname === "/ai/image" &&
    (location.rawModel == null || !isImageModelId(location.rawModel));
```

Verified: Explore from `/ai/image?model=gpt_image_2` now lands on `/` with the home feed and the `Higgsfield AI — AI-native creative suite` title. Invalid-model fallback and refresh still behave as before.

**Empty state:** it's now shared shell chrome shown for every model rather than Soul Cinema data. The card cluster media moved to `IMAGE_EMPTY_STATE_MEDIA` in `image.models.ts`, the two copy strings moved into `en.json`/`es.json` as `image.emptyStateTitle` and `image.emptyStateBody`, and the highlighted line is just the selected model's catalog label. I dropped `emptyState` from `ImageComposerConfig` entirely, so there's no per-model branch. Soul Cinema still renders exactly its captured wording because its label is `Higgsfield Soul Cinema`; GPT Image 2 reads `START CREATING WITH / GPT IMAGE 2`, Z-Image reads `START CREATING WITH / Z-IMAGE`.

Worth flagging since it reverses an earlier constraint: the four `soul-cinematic-*.webp` assets and the descriptive body line are now reused for all thirteen models on your instruction that this is common chrome. Only Soul Cinema's version is evidence-backed; the rest is the shared treatment applied to unproven models. Lint and `tsc` are clean.

GOAL
==================================================

Build one shared global SearchModal / command palette.

It opens from:

- Cmd+K on macOS
- Ctrl+K on Windows/Linux

It should reproduce the client-side search behavior found in the HAR.

There is NO search API.

FIRST: REUSE
==================================================

Inspect existing repo code for:

- global Zustand/UI state
- Header search trigger

- DropdownMenu/list components

- existing header model catalog

- badges
- Button/Input primitives

- i18n

Reuse them.

Proceed directly with implementation after this quick reuse inspection.
Do not stop for another architecture report.

==================================================
GLOBAL OWNERSHIP
==================================================

SearchModal is global UI.

Mount it ONCE at app level, alongside:

- AuthModal
- UpgradeModal

Do not mount SearchModal inside individual routes.

Prefer global UI state conceptually like:

{
  searchOpen: boolean
  openSearch()
  closeSearch()
}

If the existing global UI store already has a suitable pattern, extend it rather than adding another provider.

==================================================
SEARCH DATA ARCHITECTURE
==================================================

Create one assembled SiteSearchData registry.

Conceptually:

type SiteSearchData = {
  modelGroups: ModelGroup[]

  productItems: SearchItem[]
  productGridItems: SearchItem[]
  productPromos: ProductPromo[]

  appItems: SearchItem[]

  characterItems: SearchItem[]
  characterPromo?: ProductPromo

  communityItems: SearchItem[]
  communityPromo?: ProductPromo

  originalItems: SearchItem[]

  allItems: SearchItem[]

  recentFallbackItems: SearchItem[]
  trendingItems: SearchItem[]
}

Adapt the exact type to the existing repo.

IMPORTANT:

Do not manually duplicate model catalogs.

Derive model search data from the model registries/catalogs that already exist.

For example:

Image model registry
→ Image search items

Later:

Video model registry
→ Video search items

Audio registry
→ Audio search items

Search is only another projection of those registries.

==================================================
SEARCH ITEM
==================================================

Use a normalized leaf structure conceptually like:

type SearchItem = {
  id: string
  title: string
  description: string
  href: string

  kind: SearchKind

  modelId?: string

  generationModel?: {
    mode: string
    model: string
  }

  icon?: ReactNode
  badge?: SearchBadge

  keywords: string[]
}

Do not force this exact type if existing repo structures already provide equivalent fields.

==================================================
MODEL GROUPS
==================================================

Models contains nested groups:

- Image models
- Video models
- Edit models
- Audio models

Conceptually:

type ModelGroup = {
  id: string
  label: string
  shortLabel: string
  count: number
  icon: ReactNode
  items: SearchItem[]
}

Count should be derived from `items.length`.
Do not manually hardcode counts.

For now, use existing confirmed registries/data.

Do not invent missing Video/Audio models merely to fill groups.

An empty/unavailable group may remain absent until its registry exists.

==================================================
TOP-LEVEL CATEGORIES
==================================================

The command palette tabs are:

1. Models
2. Products
3. Characters
4. Community
5. Apps
6. Originals

Preserve this ordering.

Do not merge Products and Apps.

Apps are derived from product items whose href begins:

/apps

Products should be deduplicated by `href`.

==================================================
CHARACTER ORDER
==================================================

Use the confirmed character ordering:

1. Photo Dump
2. AI Cast
3. Character Swap
4. AI Stylist
5. Soul ID
6. AI Influencer
7. Face Swap
8. Video Face Swap
9. Recast

Use confirmed href/icon/description data already present in research or project data.

Do not invent destinations if they remain unknown.

==================================================
QUERY BEHAVIOR
==================================================

Filtering behavior from the HAR:

1. trim query
2. lowercase query
3. construct one searchable lowercase string per item from:

- title
- translated title if applicable
- description
- translated description if applicable
- kind
- keywords

Matching is:

searchableString.includes(query)

DO NOT implement:

- fuzzy matching
- Fuse.js
- ranking
- Levenshtein
- relevance scores
- server search

We are reproducing the observed behavior.

==================================================
TAB BEHAVIOR
==================================================

Important behavior:

When query is EMPTY:

selected tab filters what is shown.

Example:

Models
→ model content

Characters
→ characters

Apps
→ apps

etc.

BUT:

Once the user begins typing:

DO NOT restrict search to the selected tab.

Search across:

allItems

Then group matching results by item kind/category.

This behavior is important.

==================================================
DEBOUNCE
==================================================

Observed behavior:

query update debounce:
200ms

analytics timing:
600ms

Implement the 200ms search debounce.

For analytics:

we do not have a real analytics backend.

Do not add one.

You may implement a harmless internal/no-op callback or omit actual network tracking while preserving the timing abstraction if useful.

Do NOT make external analytics requests.

==================================================
URL STATE
==================================================

The live implementation synchronizes search state with URL search parameters.

However:

the exact parameter names could not be proven because one cached JS chunk was absent from the HAR.

Therefore:

FIRST inspect our existing router/search-param conventions.

If we already have an established global search parameter convention, reuse it.

Otherwise use a minimal clear convention such as:

?search=<query>

and optionally:

&searchTab=<tab>

ONLY if URL synchronization is worth preserving in this SPA.

Do not pretend these names are exact live Higgsfield names.

Keep the implementation isolated so parameter names can be changed easily later.

Opening/closing search should not destroy unrelated URL search params such as:

?model=

For example:

/ai/image?model=gpt_image_2

must not lose `model=gpt_image_2` when Search opens/types/closes.

==================================================
RECENTS
==================================================

Use localStorage key EXACTLY:

site-search-recents

Store only selected item IDs.

Maximum:

3

Most recent first.

When the user selects a result:

1. remove existing duplicate ID
2. prepend selected ID
3. truncate to 3
4. persist

Do not persist complete item objects.

Resolve stored IDs against current SiteSearchData.

Ignore stale IDs safely.

==================================================
IDLE STATE
==================================================

When no query exists, support the researched idle content:

- selected category/tab
- recent items
- recentFallbackItems
- trendingItems
- promos where data exists

Do not invent unknown promotional content.

If exact promos/catalog entries were not present in the HAR/research, leave them absent rather than fabricating them.

==================================================
RESULT GROUPING
==================================================

When typing:

search allItems.

Then group matching results by kind.

Render clear group headings.

Do not flatten everything into one visually undifferentiated list if the HAR shows grouped results.

==================================================
VISUAL STRUCTURE
==================================================

Build the command palette to match the captured/search research:

Conceptually:

SearchModal
├─ SearchInput
├─ top-level category tabs
├─ content
│   ├─ idle/category state
│   └─ grouped query results
└─ optional promo/trending/recent areas

For Models idle state:

Models
├─ Image
├─ Video
├─ Edit
└─ Audio

Each nested group contains model rows.

Search result row should be reusable and driven by SearchItem.

Do not create separate row components per category unless the visual structure actually differs significantly.

==================================================
KEYBOARD BEHAVIOR
==================================================

Implement:

Cmd+K
Ctrl+K

to open the global SearchModal.

Prevent the browser's default behavior where appropriate.

Do not trigger the shortcut while the user is composing text in a way that would break normal input behavior.

Also support:

Escape
→ close

If our existing Dialog primitive already handles Escape, reuse it.

Basic keyboard accessibility should include:

- focus input when opened
- arrow navigation if straightforward with existing primitives
- Enter activates selected result
- visible focus state

Do not build a huge command-menu framework solely for keyboard navigation.

==================================================
NAVIGATION
==================================================

Selecting an item should:

1. add it to recents
2. close SearchModal
3. navigate to its `href`

Use TanStack Router navigation where appropriate.

Do not use:

window.location.href

for internal routes.

Model items should use their existing canonical href.

Example Image models should route to:

/ai/image?model=<real-id>

Do not reconstruct these hrefs in Search if the existing model registry already exposes them.

==================================================
IMPORTANT: OUT-OF-SCOPE DESTINATIONS
==================================================

Search may know about products/apps whose actual destination page is outside the rebuild scope.

Do NOT build those pages just because Search references them.

Do NOT expand product scope.

If a known destination exists in our catalog but its route is intentionally not implemented, keep the catalog architecture clean.

Do not silently map it to an unrelated implemented route.

==================================================

==================================================

Do NOT reuse this global search engine for:

/apps?q=

HAR research confirmed `/apps?q=` is a separate search system based on server-loader categories:

category.apps[]

Global SearchModal and `/apps` search are different systems.

Do not merge them.

==================================================
COMMUNITY
==================================================

Important distinction:

Global search Community entries are bundled/static index items.

Community gallery pagination is server-driven and separate.

Do not add Community pagination logic to SearchModal.

==================================================
NO DUPLICATED SOURCES OF TRUTH
==================================================

header image models
+
ImageShell models
+
Search image models

as three separately maintained arrays.

Search should derive/adapt existing model data.

Where possible:

existing model registry
→ mapper

==================================================
